	try {
		let pathSegments = [];
		try {
			pathSegments = window.location.pathname.split("/").filter((segment) => segment !== "" && segment !== "global").slice(0, 2);
		} catch {
			pathSegments = [];
		}
		const matchedLang = pathSegments.map((seg) => validLangs.find((lang) => lang.toLowerCase() === seg.toLowerCase())).find((lang) => lang !== void 0);
		const candidates = [];
		if (matchedLang) candidates.push(matchedLang);
		else candidates.push(defaultLang);
		candidates.push("global");
		let content = void 0;
		let usedLang = void 0;
		for (const key of candidates) {
			if (!key) continue;
			const banner = bannerByLocale[key];
			if (!banner?.content) continue;
			content = banner.content;
			usedLang = key;
			break;
		}
		if (!content) {
			document.documentElement.setAttribute(attr, "hidden");
			return;
		}
		let shouldShow = true;
		let i = 0;

			i++;

@container/columns-container relative mt-8 prose prose-gray dark:prose-invert [contain:inline-size] isolate empty:hidden mb-14" data-page-title="Handle approvals and user input" data-page-href="/en/agent-sdk/user-input" id="content"><span data-as="p">While working on a task, Claude sometimes needs to check in with users. It might need permission before deleting files, or need to ask which database to use for a new project. Your application needs to surface these requests to users so Claude can continue with their input.</span>
<span data-as="p">Claude requests user input in two situations: when it needs <strong>permission to use a tool</strong> (like deleting files or running commands), and when it has <strong>clarifying questions</strong> (via the <code>AskUserQuestion</code> tool). Both trigger your <code>canUseTool</code> callback, which pauses execution until you return a response. This is different from normal conversation turns where Claude finishes and waits for your next message.</span>
<span data-as="p">For clarifying questions, Claude generates the questions and options. Your role is to present them to users and return their selections. You can’t add your own questions to this flow; if you need to ask users something yourself, do that separately in your application logic.</span>
<span data-as="p">The callback can stay pending indefinitely. Execution remains paused until your callback returns. If a user might take longer to respond than your process can reasonably stay running, register a <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/hooks" class="link"><code>PreToolUse</code> hook</a> that returns the <a href="/docs/en/hooks#defer-a-tool-call-for-later" class="link"><code>defer</code> decision</a> instead of waiting in the callback, so the process can exit and resume later from the persisted session.</span>
<span data-as="p">This guide shows you how to detect each type of request and respond appropriately.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="detect-when-claude-needs-input" aria-labelledby="_R_b9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#detect-when-claude-needs-input" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Detect when Claude needs input"></a><span id="_R_b9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Detect when Claude needs input</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Pass a <code>canUseTool</code> callback in your query options. The callback fires whenever Claude needs user input, receiving the tool name and input as arguments:</span>

<span data-as="p">The callback fires in two cases:</span>
<ol>
<li><strong>Tool needs approval</strong>: Claude makes a call that the <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/permissions#how-permissions-are-evaluated" class="link">permission flow</a> hasn’t already approved or denied. Check <code>tool_name</code> for the tool (for example, <code>&quot;Bash&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;Write&quot;</code>).</li>
<li><strong>Claude asks a question</strong>: Claude calls the <code>AskUserQuestion</code> tool. Check if <code>tool_name == &quot;AskUserQuestion&quot;</code> to handle it differently. If you specify a <code>tools</code> array, include <code>AskUserQuestion</code> for this to work. For more information, see <a href="#handle-clarifying-questions" class="link">Handle clarifying questions</a>.</li>
</ol>
<div role="note" aria-label="Warning" class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-yellow-200 bg-yellow-50 dark:border-yellow-900 dark:bg-yellow-600/20 [&amp;_[data-component-part=&#x27;callout-icon&#x27;]]:mt-px" data-callout-type="warning"><div class="mt-0.5 w-4" data-component-part="callout-icon"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="1.5" class="flex-none size-5 text-yellow-800 dark:text-yellow-300"><use href="#mi-mint-1-CalloutWarning"></use></svg></div><div class="text-sm prose dark:prose-invert min-w-0 w-full [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:text-current! [&amp;_kbd]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-yellow-800 dark:text-yellow-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p"><strong>An earlier step can allow or deny a call before <code>canUseTool</code> runs.</strong> The <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/permissions#how-permissions-are-evaluated" class="link">permission evaluation flow</a> checks deny rules, the permission mode, and allow rules before your callback.</span><span data-as="p">To check calls that run without your callback, such as file reads inside your <a href="/docs/en/permissions#working-directories" class="link">working directories</a>, use a <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/hooks" class="link"><code>PreToolUse</code> hook</a>, which can allow, deny, or modify them.</span></div></div>
<span data-as="p">You can also use the <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/hooks#available-hooks" class="link"><code>PermissionRequest</code> hook</a> to send external notifications (Slack, email, push) when Claude is waiting for approval.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="handle-tool-approval-requests" aria-labelledby="_R_p9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#handle-tool-approval-requests" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Handle tool approval requests"></a><span id="_R_p9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Handle tool approval requests</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Once you’ve passed a <code>canUseTool</code> callback in your query options, it fires when Claude wants to use a tool that nothing earlier in the permission flow has approved. In some configurations, such as <code>dontAsk</code> mode, Claude Code doesn’t call it; the last step of <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/permissions#how-permissions-are-evaluated" class="link">How permissions are evaluated</a> lists them and says what happens to the call instead.</span>
<span data-as="p">Your callback receives three arguments:</span>
<!-- --><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" data-table-wrapper="true" class="min-w-0 [--page-padding:20px] flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-(--page-padding) max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div data-id="base-ui-_R_v9deehkd74t9_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport" role="region" aria-label="Scrollable table"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="flex"><div class="px-(--page-padding) grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px]"><thead><tr><th>Argument</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>toolName</code></td><td>The name of the tool Claude wants to use (for example, <code>&quot;Bash&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;Write&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;Edit&quot;</code>)</td></tr><tr><td><code>input</code></td><td>The parameters Claude is passing to the tool. Contents vary by tool.</td></tr><tr><td><code>options</code> (TS) / <code>context</code> (Python)</td><td>Additional context including optional <code>suggestions</code> (proposed <code>PermissionUpdate</code> entries to avoid re-prompting) and a cancellation signal. In TypeScript, <code>signal</code> is an <code>AbortSignal</code>; in Python, the signal field is reserved for future use. See <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/python#toolpermissioncontext" class="link"><code>ToolPermissionContext</code></a> for Python.</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p">The <code>input</code> object contains tool-specific parameters. Common examples:</span>
<!-- --><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" data-table-wrapper="true" class="min-w-0 [--page-padding:20px] flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-(--page-padding) max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div data-id="base-ui-_R_139deehkd74t9_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport" role="region" aria-label="Scrollable table"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="flex"><div class="px-(--page-padding) grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px]"><thead><tr><th>Tool</th><th>Input fields</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>Bash</code></td><td><code>command</code>, <code>description</code>, <code>timeout</code></td></tr><tr><td><code>Write</code></td><td><code>file_path</code>, <code>content</code></td></tr><tr><td><code>Edit</code></td><td><code>file_path</code>, <code>old_string</code>, <code>new_string</code></td></tr><tr><td><code>Read</code></td><td><code>file_path</code>, <code>offset</code>, <code>limit</code></td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p">See the SDK reference for complete input schemas: <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/python#tool-input%2Foutput-types" class="link">Python</a> | <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/typescript#tool-input-types" class="link">TypeScript</a>.</span>
<span data-as="p">You can display this information to the user so they can decide whether to allow or reject the action, then return the appropriate response.</span>
<span data-as="p">The following example asks Claude to create and delete a test file. When Claude attempts each operation, the callback prints the tool request to the terminal and prompts for y/n approval.</span>

<span data-as="p">This example uses a <code>y/n</code> flow where any input other than <code>y</code> is treated as a denial. In practice, you might build a richer UI that lets users modify the request, provide feedback, or redirect Claude entirely. See <a href="#respond-to-tool-requests" class="link">Respond to tool requests</a> for all the ways you can respond.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="respond-to-tool-requests" aria-labelledby="_R_1f9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#respond-to-tool-requests" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Respond to tool requests"></a><span id="_R_1f9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Respond to tool requests</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Your callback returns one of two response types:</span>
<!-- --><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" data-table-wrapper="true" class="min-w-0 [--page-padding:20px] flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-(--page-padding) max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div data-id="base-ui-_R_1j9deehkd74t9_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport" role="region" aria-label="Scrollable table"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="flex"><div class="px-(--page-padding) grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px]"><thead><tr><th>Response</th><th>Python</th><th>TypeScript</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><strong>Allow</strong></td><td><code>PermissionResultAllow(updated_input=...)</code></td><td><code>{ behavior: &quot;allow&quot;, updatedInput }</code></td></tr><tr><td><strong>Deny</strong></td><td><code>PermissionResultDeny(message=...)</code></td><td><code>{ behavior: &quot;deny&quot;, message }</code></td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p">When allowing, the tool runs with the input Claude requested unless you return a modified input, <code>updatedInput</code> in TypeScript or <code>updated_input</code> in Python. Before v2.1.207, Claude Code rejected an allow result that omitted <code>updatedInput</code> and denied the tool call with a validation error.</span>
<span data-as="p">When denying, provide a message explaining why. Claude sees this message and may adjust its approach.</span>
<span data-as="p">Beyond allowing or denying, you can modify the tool’s input or provide context that helps Claude adjust its approach:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong>Approve</strong>: let the tool execute as Claude requested</li>
<li><strong>Approve with changes</strong>: modify the input before execution (for example, sanitize paths, add constraints)</li>
<li><strong>Approve and remember</strong>: echo a suggested permission rule back so matching calls skip the prompt next time</li>
<li><strong>Reject</strong>: block the tool and tell Claude why</li>
<li><strong>Suggest alternative</strong>: block but guide Claude toward what the user wants instead</li>
<li><strong>Redirect entirely</strong>: use <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/streaming-vs-single-mode" class="link">streaming input</a> to send Claude a completely new instruction</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">The <code>ask_user</code> and <code>askUser</code> helpers in the following snippets stand in for your application’s own prompt UI.</span>

</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#ffffff;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div></div></div></div><div id="panel-redirect-entirely-5" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="redirect-entirely" tabindex="0" class="prose dark:prose-invert overflow-x-auto [&amp;_[data-table-wrapper]]:![--page-padding:0px] [&amp;_[role=&quot;listitem&quot;]]:pl-4 -ml-4 pl-4 [&amp;&gt;:first-child:not(p)]:mt-0 [&amp;&gt;:first-child:not(p)_img]:mt-0 [&amp;&gt;:first-child[data-table-wrapper]]:pt-0! hidden" data-component-part="tab-content"><span data-as="p">For a complete change of direction (not just a nudge), use <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/streaming-vs-single-mode" class="link">streaming input</a> to send Claude a new instruction directly. This bypasses the current tool request and gives Claude entirely new instructions to follow.</span></div></div></div>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="handle-clarifying-questions" aria-labelledby="_R_219deehkd74t9_"><a href="#handle-clarifying-questions" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Handle clarifying questions"></a><span id="_R_219deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Handle clarifying questions</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">When Claude needs more direction on a task with multiple valid approaches, it calls the <code>AskUserQuestion</code> tool. This triggers your <code>canUseTool</code> callback with <code>toolName</code> set to <code>AskUserQuestion</code>. The input contains Claude’s questions as multiple-choice options, which you display to the user and return their selections.</span>
<div role="note" aria-label="Tip" class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-green-200 bg-green-50 dark:border-green-900 dark:bg-green-600/20 [&amp;_[data-component-part=&#x27;callout-icon&#x27;]]:mt-px" data-callout-type="tip"><div class="mt-0.5 w-4" data-component-part="callout-icon"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="1.5" class="size-4.5 text-green-800 dark:text-green-300"><use href="#mi-mint-1-CalloutTip"></use></svg></div><div class="text-sm prose dark:prose-invert min-w-0 w-full [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:text-current! [&amp;_kbd]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-green-800 dark:text-green-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p">Clarifying questions are especially common in <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/permissions#plan-mode-plan" class="link"><code>plan</code> mode</a>, where Claude explores the codebase and asks questions before proposing a plan. This makes plan mode ideal for interactive workflows where you want Claude to gather requirements before making changes.</span></div></div>
<span data-as="p">The following steps show how to handle clarifying questions:</span>
<div role="list" class="steps ml-3.5 mt-10 mb-6"><div role="listitem" class="step group/step step-container relative flex items-start pb-5"><div data-component-part="step-line" class="absolute w-px h-[calc(100%-2.75rem)] top-11 bg-gray-200/70 dark:bg-white/10" contentEditable="false"></div><div class="absolute ml-[-13px] py-2" data-component-part="step-number" contentEditable="false"><div data-component-part="step-indicator" class="group/step-indicator relative size-7 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-gray-50 dark:bg-white/10 text-xs text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-50 font-semibold flex items-center justify-center"><div class="[[data-component-part=step-indicator]:has(a:focus-visible)_&amp;]:opacity-0">1</div></div></div><div class="w-full overflow-hidden pl-8 pr-px"><p class="mt-2 font-semibold prose dark:prose-invert text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-200" contentEditable="false" data-component-part="step-title">Pass a canUseTool callback</p><div data-component-part="step-content" class="prose dark:prose-invert"><span data-as="p">Pass a <code>canUseTool</code> callback in your query options. By default, <code>AskUserQuestion</code> is available. If you specify a <code>tools</code> array to restrict Claude’s capabilities (for example, a read-only agent with only <code>Read</code>, <code>Glob</code>, and <code>Grep</code>), include <code>AskUserQuestion</code> in that array. Otherwise, Claude won’t be able to ask clarifying questions:</span><div data-orientation="horizontal" data-activation-direction="none" class="code-group p-0.5 mt-5 mb-8 flex flex-col not-prose <h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="question-format" aria-labelledby="_R_2b9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#question-format" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Question format"></a><span id="_R_2b9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Question format</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">The input contains Claude’s generated questions in a <code>questions</code> array. Each question has these fields:</span>
<!-- --><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" data-table-wrapper="true" class="min-w-0 [--page-padding:20px] flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-(--page-padding) max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div data-id="base-ui-_R_2f9deehkd74t9_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport" role="region" aria-label="Scrollable table"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="flex"><div class="px-(--page-padding) grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px]"><thead><tr><th>Field</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>question</code></td><td>The full question text to display</td></tr><tr><td><code>header</code></td><td>Short label for the question (max 12 characters)</td></tr><tr><td><code>options</code></td><td>Array of 2-4 choices, each with <code>label</code> and <code>description</code>. TypeScript: optionally <code>preview</code>. See <a href="#option-previews-typescript" class="link">Option previews</a>.</td></tr><tr><td><code>multiSelect</code></td><td>If <code>true</code>, users can select multiple options</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p">The structure your callback receives:</span>

<h4 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="option-previews-typescript" aria-labelledby="_R_2l9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#option-previews-typescript" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Option previews (TypeScript)"></a><span id="_R_2l9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Option previews (TypeScript)</span></h4>
<span data-as="p"><code>toolConfig.askUserQuestion.previewFormat</code> adds a <code>preview</code> field to each option so your app can show a visual mockup alongside the label. Without this setting, Claude does not generate previews and the field is absent.</span>
<!-- --><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" data-table-wrapper="true" class="min-w-0 [--page-padding:20px] flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-(--page-padding) max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div data-id="base-ui-_R_2p9deehkd74t9_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport" role="region" aria-label="Scrollable table"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="flex"><div class="px-(--page-padding) grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px]"><thead><tr><th style="text-align:left"><code>previewFormat</code></th><th style="text-align:left"><code>preview</code> contains</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td style="text-align:left">unset (default)</td><td style="text-align:left">Field is absent. Claude does not generate previews.</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>&quot;markdown&quot;</code></td><td style="text-align:left">ASCII art and fenced code blocks</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align:left"><code>&quot;html&quot;</code></td><td style="text-align:left">A styled <code>&lt;div&gt;</code> fragment (the SDK rejects <code>&lt;script&gt;</code>, <code>&lt;style&gt;</code>, and <code>&lt;!DOCTYPE&gt;</code> before your callback runs)</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p">The format applies to all questions in the session. Claude includes <code>preview</code> on options where a visual comparison helps (layout choices, color schemes) and omits it where one wouldn’t (yes/no confirmations, text-only choices). Check for <code>undefined</code> before rendering.</span>

<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="response-format" aria-labelledby="_R_339deehkd74t9_"><a href="#response-format" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Response format"></a><span id="_R_339deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Response format</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Return an <code>answers</code> object mapping each question’s <code>question</code> field to the selected option’s <code>label</code>:</span>
<!-- --><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" data-table-wrapper="true" class="min-w-0 [--page-padding:20px] flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-(--page-padding) max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div data-id="base-ui-_R_379deehkd74t9_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport" role="region" aria-label="Scrollable table"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="flex"><div class="px-(--page-padding) grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px]"><thead><tr><th>Field</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>questions</code></td><td>Pass through the original questions array (required for tool processing)</td></tr><tr><td><code>answers</code></td><td>Object where keys are question text and values are selected labels</td></tr><tr><td><code>response</code></td><td>Optional freeform reply the user typed instead of answering the structured questions</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div>
<span data-as="p">For multi-select questions, pass an array of labels or join them with <code>&quot;, &quot;</code>. For per-question free text such as an “Other” option, put the user’s text in <code>answers[question]</code> as shown in <a href="#support-free-text-input" class="link">Support free-text input</a>. Set <code>response</code> only when your UI lets the user dismiss the question card and type a general reply that isn’t an answer to any specific question. When <code>response</code> is set, Claude receives “The user responded: …” instead of the per-question answer list.</span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#116329;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">    &quot;How should I format the output?&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;Summary&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">}</span></span>

<h4 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="support-free-text-input" aria-labelledby="_R_3d9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#support-free-text-input" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Support free-text input"></a><span id="_R_3d9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Support free-text input</span></h4>
<span data-as="p">Claude’s predefined options won’t always cover what users want. To let users type their own answer:</span>
<ul>
<li>Display an additional “Other” choice after Claude’s options that accepts text input</li>
<li>Use the user’s custom text as the answer value (not the word “Other”)</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">See the <a href="#complete-example" class="link">complete example</a> below for a full implementation.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="complete-example" aria-labelledby="_R_3l9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#complete-example" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Complete example"></a><span id="_R_3l9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Complete example</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Claude asks clarifying questions when it needs user input to proceed. For example, when asked to help decide on a tech stack for a mobile app, Claude might ask about cross-platform vs native, backend preferences, or target platforms. These questions help Claude make decisions that match the user’s preferences rather than guessing.</span>
<span data-as="p">This example handles those questions in a terminal application. Here’s what happens at each step:</span>
<ol>
<li><strong>Route the request</strong>: The <code>canUseTool</code> callback checks if the tool name is <code>&quot;AskUserQuestion&quot;</code> and routes to a dedicated handler</li>
<li><strong>Display questions</strong>: The handler loops through the <code>questions</code> array and prints each question with numbered options</li>
<li><strong>Collect input</strong>: The user can enter a number to select an option, or type free text directly (for example, “jquery”, “i don’t know”)</li>
<li><strong>Map answers</strong>: The code checks if input is numeric (uses the option’s label) or free text (uses the text directly)</li>
<li><strong>Return to Claude</strong>: The response includes both the original <code>questions</code> array and the <code>answers</code> mapping</li>
</ol>
<span data-as="p">Save the TypeScript version as <code>ask.ts</code> and run it with <code>npx tsx ask.ts</code>, or save the Python version as <code>ask.py</code> and run it with <code>python ask.py</code>.</span>

<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="limitations" aria-labelledby="_R_419deehkd74t9_"><a href="#limitations" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Limitations"></a><span id="_R_419deehkd74t9_" class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Limitations</span></h2>
<ul>
<li><strong>Subagents</strong>: <code>AskUserQuestion</code> is not currently available in subagents spawned via the Agent tool</li>
<li><strong>Question limits</strong>: each <code>AskUserQuestion</code> call supports 1-4 questions with 2-4 options each</li>
</ul>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="other-ways-to-get-user-input" aria-labelledby="_R_459deehkd74t9_"><a href="#other-ways-to-get-user-input" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Other ways to get user input"></a><span id="_R_459deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Other ways to get user input</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">The <code>canUseTool</code> callback and <code>AskUserQuestion</code> tool cover most approval and clarification scenarios, but the SDK offers other ways to get input from users:</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="streaming-input" aria-labelledby="_R_499deehkd74t9_"><a href="#streaming-input" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Streaming input"></a><span id="_R_499deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Streaming input</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Use <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/streaming-vs-single-mode" class="link">streaming input</a> when you need to:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong>Interrupt the agent mid-task</strong>: send a cancel signal or change direction while Claude is working</li>
<li><strong>Provide additional context</strong>: add information Claude needs without waiting for it to ask</li>
<li><strong>Build chat interfaces</strong>: let users send follow-up messages during long-running operations</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">Streaming input is ideal for conversational UIs where users interact with the agent throughout execution, not just at approval checkpoints.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="custom-tools" aria-labelledby="_R_4h9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#custom-tools" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Custom tools"></a><span id="_R_4h9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Custom tools</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Use <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/custom-tools" class="link">custom tools</a> when you need to:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong>Collect structured input</strong>: build forms, wizards, or multi-step workflows that go beyond <code>AskUserQuestion</code>’s multiple-choice format</li>
<li><strong>Integrate external approval systems</strong>: connect to existing ticketing, workflow, or approval platforms</li>
<li><strong>Implement domain-specific interactions</strong>: create tools tailored to your application’s needs, like code review interfaces or deployment checklists</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">Custom tools give you full control over the interaction, but require more implementation work than using the built-in <code>canUseTool</code> callback.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="related-resources" aria-labelledby="_R_4p9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#related-resources" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Related resources"></a><span id="_R_4p9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Related resources</span></h2>
<ul>
<li><a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/permissions" class="link">Configure permissions</a>: set up permission modes and rules</li>
<li><a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/hooks" class="link">Control execution with hooks</a>: run custom code at key points in the agent lifecycle</li>
<li><a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/typescript#canusetool" class="link">TypeScript SDK reference</a>: full canUseTool API documentation</li>
